use |\n\n### Key Decisions\n1. **Retain only the 44 code-selected ALM sessions present in `data/`**: This is the cleanest way to match the paper’s analyzed dataset rather than the broader raw archive.\n2. **Use the ALM-designated probe only for each retained session**: Raw files can contain multiple probes/areas; the reference loaders specify which probe corresponds to ALM.\n3. **Exclude stimulation, early-lick, and ignore/no-response trials**: Reference analyses consistently use `~stim.enable`, `~early`, and usually hit/miss conditions; ignore trials are omitted in the paper.\n4. **Use `goCue` as the universal alignment event**: This matches the user request and the default reference alignment.\n5. **Use a fixed window of `[-2.5, 2.5] s` around `goCue` with a 5 ms bin (`dt = 1/200`)**: This matches the default reference processing pipeline better than the 10 ms tutorial example and gives a shared time axis for neural/video streams.\n6. **Use all non-garbage manually curated units on the selected probe, then apply a 1 Hz FR threshold**: This matches the paper’s “all units >1 Hz” rule for most analyses better than a single-unit-only subset.\n7. **Represent trial-level categorical outputs as constant time series**: This keeps all outputs on the same `(n_output, n_timepoints)` grid while preserving the per-trial labels requested by the user.\n8. **Define tongue velocity from the side-view `tongue` marker speed magnitude**: This uses a direct reference-processed kinematic channel without inventing an unreferenced cross-camera combination.\n9. **Define paw velocity from the average of top- and bottom-paw speed magnitudes in the bottom view**: This captures overall paw movement while remaining close to the tracked features described in the paper/code.\n10. **Use per-session 50th-percentile thresholds for tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy**: This follows the decoder task even where it differs from the paper’s manual np.zeros((n_time,), dtype=np.float64)\n            basederiv = np.array([0.0, 0.0], dtype=np.float64)\n        else:\n            if n_time > 1:\n                deriv = np.diff(tsinterp, axis=0)\n convert_one_session(spec: SessionSpec, show_processing: bool, outdir: Path) -> dict | None:\n   700\t    t0 = time.time()\n   701\t    raw = load_session(spec)\n   702\t    valid = min(raw[\"R\"].size, max(covered_trial_max))\n   712\t        selected_trials = selected_trials[selected_trials + 1 <= max_neural_trial]\n   713\t\n   714\t    if selected_trials.size < 2:\n   715\t        log(f\"SKIP {spec.session_id}: only {selected_trials.size} valid hit/miss non-stim non-early trials\")\n   716\t        return None\n   717\t\n   718\t    time_edges = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT, DT, dtype=np.float64)\n   719\t    time_vec = time_edges[:-1] + DT / 2.0\n   720\t\n   721\t    trial_to_pos = np.full(raw[\"R\"].size, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   722\t    trial_to_pos[selected_trials] = np.arange(selected_trials.size, dtype=np.int64)\n   723\t\n   724\t    kept_units = 0\n   725\t    neural_trials = [[] for _ in range(selected_trials.size)]\n   726\t    for unit in raw[\"units\"]:\n   727\t        if not good_quality(unit[\"quality\"]):\n   728\t            continue\n   729\t        unit_mat = compute_unit_trial_matrix(unit, raw[\"events\"][\"goCue\"], trial_to_pos, selected_trials.size, time_edges)\n   730\t        if float(unit_mat.mean()) <= LOW_FR_HZ:\n   731\t            continue\n   732\t        kept_units += 1\n   733\t        unit_mat = unit_mat.astype(np.float32)\n   734\t        for trix in range(selected_trials.size):\n   735\t            neural_trials[trix].append(unit_mat[trix])\n   736\t\n   737\t    if kept_units < 10:\n   738\t        log(f\"SKIP {spec.session_id}: only {kept_units} units after quality/FR filtering\")\n   739\t        return None\n   740\t\n   741\t    tongue_pos = feature_xy(raw, 0, \"tongue\", raw[\"events\"][\"goCue\"], time_vec)\n   742\t    tongue_speed = feature_speed(*tongue_pos, \"tongue\")\n   743\t    tongue_valid = np.isfinite(tongue_pos[0]) & np.isfinite(tongue_pos[1])\n   744\t    tongue_speed = tongue_speed.astype(np.float64, copy=False)\n   745\t    tongue_speed[~tongue_valid] = np.nan\n   746\t\n   747\t    paw_speeds = []\n   748\t    for paw_name in (\"top_paw\", \"bottom_paw\"):\n   749\t        paw_pos = feature_xy(raw, 1, paw_name, raw[\"events\"][\"goCue\"], time_vec)\n   750\t        paw_speeds.append(feature_speed(*paw_pos, paw_name))\n   751\t    paw_stack = np.stack(paw_speeds, axis=0)\n   752\t    paw_count = np.sum(np.isfinite(paw_stack), axis=0)\n   753\t    paw_sum = np.nansum(paw_stack, axis=0)\n   754\t    paw_speed = np.divide(paw_sum, np.maximum(paw_count, 1), where=np.maximum(paw_count, 1) > 0)\n   755\t    paw_speed = np.nan_to_num(paw_speed, nan=0.0)\n   756\t\n   757\t    motion = aligned_motion_energy(raw, raw[\"events\"][\"goCue\"], time_vec)\n   758\t    motion = np.nan_to_num(motion, nan=0.0)\n   759\t\n   760\t    tongue_sel = tongue_speed[:, selected_trials]\n   761\t    paw_sel = paw_speed[:, selected_trials]\n   762\t    motion_sel = motion[:, selected_trials]\n   763\t\n   764\t    tongue_thr = summarize_threshold(tongue_sel)\n   765\t    paw_thr = summarize_threshold(paw_sel)\n   766\t    motion_thr = summarize_threshold(motion_sel)\n   767\t\n   768\t    input_trials = []\n   769\t    output_trials = []\n   770\t    final_neural = []\n   771\t    for local_idx, trial_idx in input_trials.append(time_vec[None, :].astype(np.float32))\n   775\t\n   776\t        lick_direction = np.int64(1 if raw[\"R\"][trial_idx] == 1 else 0)\n   777\t        context = np.int64(1 if          np.full(time_vec.size, lick_direction, dtype=np.int64),\n   782\t                np.full(time_vec.size, context, dtype=np.int64),\n   783\t                np.full(time_vec.size, outcome,                  0,\n   788\t                ).astype(np.int64),\n   789\t                (paw_sel[:, local_idx] >= paw_thr).astype(np.int64),\n   790\t                (motion_sel[:, local_idx] >=
